Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The video operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 48. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 929 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 48 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the video structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 929. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
